# Android parity benchmark 2026-10-22

Device: sdk_gphone64_x86_64 (API 35), WebView com.google.android.webview, 154.0.8037.106. Toggle samples: 30. Emission runs: 200. Independent trials: 5.

APK policy: distinct application IDs dev.ngrxsqlite.harness.classic and dev.ngrxsqlite.harness.sqlite; each APK installed once before trials. --skip-build reuses .tmp/benchmark-apks/<impl>.apk. Trial implementation order alternates; each size starts from a cleared app and a fresh process. Inactive implementation is force-stopped. RTT is the same-session echo p50 averaged across trials; bandwidth is the average executeSet throughput fit. Report retains sample SD and all raw probes. No meminfo peak polling during timed startup.

## Phase 15d implementation, pipeline and limits

Session: `15d-final-20261022-03`. Both APKs were uninstalled before building. The Pixel_9_Pro emulator was already running; it was rebooted before this clean pipeline and again immediately before statistical trials. No tests or builds ran alongside the accepted controls/trials. The earlier session `15d-final-20261022-02` overlapped host checks and is excluded. All thirty prescribed observations are retained; none was trimmed. APK hashes, device/API/WebView, date and session match across floor, empty and workload data.

Reproduce the four stages with a fresh session S and explicit output paths:

```text
node tools/android-bench.mjs --build-only true --session S
node tools/android-floor-controls.mjs --session S --output FLOORS.json
node tools/android-parity-profile.mjs --empty --session S --output EMPTY.json
# Reboot emulator and await completed boot before the statistical stage.
node tools/android-bench.mjs --trials 5 --skip-build true --session S --floors-json FLOORS.json --platform-json EMPTY.json --output REPORT.md
```

Implemented [ADR-0057](../decisions/0057-post-write-memory-floor.md) metric-scoped PSS acceptance; [ADR-0058](../decisions/0058-startup-preload-batching.md) independent root preload batching; and [ADR-0059](../decisions/0059-canonical-entity-bulk-json.md) reuse of validated canonical bulk JSON. The obsolete floor test fixture now models the native consistency check and actual executeSet cleanup. No public store API/schema or harness application behavior changed.

All **11 remaining FAILs** are evidenced individually in [ADR-0060](../decisions/0060-phase15d-residual-overhead.md); they remain FAIL. The table below retains every control allowance and excess. This is not an all-metrics parity claim. Current counts are 13 PASS / 9 PLATFORM-BOUND / 0 BRIDGE-BOUND / 11 FAIL. Large classic SDs account for some startup PASS/bound labels; no end-to-end latency improvement is established by cross-session comparison.

The [bundle audit](android-bundle-15d-2026-10-22.json) shows +101845 initial JS bytes versus classic (+772 versus the prior delta); optional web/sql.js/devtools/spike code remains outside the initial graph. The final [1k heap comparison](android-heap-15d-after-2026-10-22.json) shows +970049 shallow bytes, including +821736 code bytes. Snapshot sums are not retained memory or PSS. Query/bulk code used before post-seed measurement cannot be made absent at that measurement just by replacing its import syntax.

The final [1k](android-toggle-trace-2026-10-22-phase15d-after-1000.json) and [10k](android-toggle-trace-2026-10-22-phase15d-after-10000.json) toggle traces and CPU profiles show one todo UPDATE commit and one follow-up effect UI commit, without entity reads or query refresh transports. The post-commit DOM span remains integration work. Seed [10k](android-seed-trace-10000-2026-10-22-phase15d-after.json), [50k CPU/native-call](android-seed-trace-50000-2026-10-22-phase15d-after.json), [native-clock](android-seed-trace-50000-2026-10-22-phase15d-after-native.json), [index](android-index-probe-15d-2026-10-22.json), and startup [1k](android-startup-15d-after-1000-2026-10-22.json)/[50k](android-startup-15d-after-50000-2026-10-22.json) diagnostics were collected separately after acceptance. Each CPU trace has a same-stem .cpuprofile sidecar. Diagnostics are not acceptance observations or extra floors; profiler/native-clock overhead and emulator drift limit before/after attribution.

The F_seed memory diagnostic is one pre/post app-PSS bracket around five latency repetitions per N, not five independent memory observations. ADR-0057 uses its pre-shrink growth, scoped only to pssTotalMb, combined with empty_delta using max, not addition. Heap and relaunch PSS keep ADR-0048.

Validation: `npx.cmd ng test ngrx-sqlite-store --watch=false` 322/322; `node --test tools/*.test.mjs` 13/13; `npx.cmd ng test harness --watch=false` 1/1; `npx.cmd playwright test` 26/26, clean exit 0. Library and both production/APK builds pass. Changes remain uncommitted. CLAUDE.md is edited in place; its Phase 15d tick is the final task step.
